34. GitHub

34.4 Code Review Middle

GitHub'da review: qatorga izoh, bir nechta qator tanlash, "suggestion" bloklari, review holatlari (Comment, Approve, Request changes), fayllarni "viewed" deb belgilash, suhbatlarni hal qilish. CODEOWNERS orqali mas'ul shaxslar avtomatik so'raladi.

Samarali review: kontekst (PR tavsifi, tiket), avtomatlashtirilgan tekshiruvlar (CI, statik tahlil) uslub masalalarini olib tashlaydi, izohlar aniq va sabab bilan, "nit:" prefiksi kichik takliflar uchun, tez javob vaqti (masalan, 1 ish kunida).

[suggestion bloki]
$order = $request->user()->orders()->findOrFail($id);

IDOR: boshqa foydalanuvchi buyurtmasiga kirishni oldini olish uchun doira bilan qidiring.

34.7 GitHub Actions Middle

GitHub Actions — repozitoriyga o'rnatilgan CI/CD: .github/workflows/*.yml da workflow'lar, event'lar (push, pull_request, schedule, workflow_dispatch), job'lar va step'lar, GitHub-hosted yoki self-hosted runner'lar, marketplace action'lari.

Laravel uchun tipik pipeline: PHP o'rnatish (shivammathur/setup-php), composer kesh, MySQL/Redis service'lar, Pint, Larastan, Pest, keyin deploy. Batafsil — GitHub Actions bo'limida.

on: [pull_request]
jobs:
  test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: shivammathur/setup-php@v2
        with: { php-version: '8.3', coverage: none }
      - run: composer install --no-interaction --prefer-dist
      - run: php artisan test --parallel
34.8 Secrets Middle

Secret'lar — shifrlangan maxfiy qiymatlar (API kalitlari, deploy kalitlari): repo, environment va organization darajasida. Workflow'da ${{ secrets.NAME }}, log'larda avtomatik niqoblanadi. Fork'dan kelgan PR'larda secret'lar berilmaydi (xavfsizlik).

Qoidalar: minimal ko'rinish (environment secret'lari faqat tegishli deploy job'ida), muntazam aylantirish, uzoq muddatli bulut kalitlari o'rniga OIDC, secret'ni echo qilmaslik yoki fayl sifatida artefaktga qo'ymaslik. Push protection + secret scanning repozitoriyga tushgan kalitlarni topadi.

- run: ./deploy.sh
  env:
    DEPLOY_KEY: ${{ secrets.DEPLOY_KEY }}

Intervyu savollari

  • middle Main branch'ni qanday himoya qilasiz? Branch protection va CODEOWNERS nima beradi?

35. GitLab

35.3 Code Review Middle

GitLab review: qatorga izoh, ko'p qatorli tanlash, suggestion'lar (bir yoki bir nechta qatorga taklif — muallif bir tugma bilan qo'llaydi), "Start a review" (izohlarni yig'ib, birdan yuborish), thread'larni resolve qilish (merge uchun majburiy qilish mumkin), approve.

Code Owners (CODEOWNERS) va approval qoidalari (35.16) review'ni majburiy qiladi. CI natijalari (testlar, Code Quality, SAST) MR'da ko'rinib, review'ni avtomatlashtirilgan tekshiruvlar bilan boyitadi.

[suggestion bloki]
$order = $request->user()->orders()->findOrFail($id);
35.6 GitLab CI/CD Middle

GitLab CI/CD — .gitlab-ci.yml orqali sozlanadigan o'rnatilgan pipeline tizimi: stage'lar, job'lar, runner'lar, artefakt va kesh, environment'lar, MR pipeline'lari, rejali pipeline'lar, include bilan qayta ishlatiladigan shablonlar, CI/CD component'lar.

Laravel uchun tipik pipeline: build (composer), test (Pint, Larastan, Pest — MySQL/Redis services bilan), deploy (staging avtomatik, production qo'lda). Batafsil — GitLab CI/CD bo'limida.

stages: [build, test, deploy]
test:
  stage: test
  image: php:8.3
  services: [mysql:8.4, redis:7]
  script:
    - composer install --no-interaction --prefer-dist
    - php artisan test --parallel
35.8 CI Variables Middle

CI/CD o'zgaruvchilari: project/group/instance darajasida, protected (faqat protected branch/tag pipeline'larida), masked (log'da yashiriladi), hidden, environment scope (production uchun alohida qiymat), "File" turi (sertifikat, kubeconfig — vaqtinchalik faylga yoziladi). Oldindan belgilangan o'zgaruvchilar: CI_COMMIT_SHA, CI_COMMIT_REF_NAME, CI_REGISTRY_IMAGE, CI_PIPELINE_SOURCE.

Production secret'lari — protected + masked + environment scope. .gitlab-ci.yml da hech qachon sir yozmang.

deploy_production:
  environment: production
  script:
    - echo "$SSH_PRIVATE_KEY" > /tmp/key && chmod 600 /tmp/key   # File turi afzal
    - ./deploy.sh "$CI_COMMIT_SHA"

Server va infratuzilma

36. Docker

36.4 Layers va Caching Middle

Har bir RUN, COPY, ADD — yangi qatlam; Docker qatlamlarni keshlaydi va instruksiya hamda uning kirishlari o'zgarmasa qayta ishlatadi. Bir qatlam o'zgarsa, undan keyingilari qayta quriladi.

Tartib qoidasi: kam o'zgaradigan narsalar yuqorida (tizim paketlari, PHP kengaytmalari), keyin dependency manifestlari (composer.json, composer.lock → composer install), eng oxirida tez-tez o'zgaradigan kod. Shunda kod o'zgarganda composer install keshdan olinadi. BuildKit kesh mount'lari (--mount=type=cache) composer/npm keshini build'lar orasida saqlaydi.

COPY composer.json composer.lock ./
RUN --mount=type=cache,target=/root/.composer/cache \
    composer install --no-dev --no-scripts --no-autoloader --prefer-dist
COPY . .
RUN composer dump-autoload --optimize --classmap-authoritative
36.13 Multi-Stage Builds Middle

Multi-stage build — bir Dockerfile'da bir nechta FROM: build bosqichlarida kompilyator, Composer, Node va dev vositalari ishlatiladi, yakuniy image'ga faqat natija (vendor/, public/build) COPY --from bilan ko'chiriladi. Natija — kichik, xavfsiz production image.

Laravel uchun odatiy bosqichlar: composer (PHP dependency'lar), node (Vite build), runtime (PHP-FPM + kod + natijalar). --target bilan dev image'ni ham shu fayldan olish mumkin.

FROM composer:2 AS vendor
COPY composer.* ./
RUN composer install --no-dev --no-scripts --prefer-dist

FROM node:22-alpine AS assets
COPY package*.json vite.config.js ./
COPY resources ./resources
RUN npm ci && npm run build

FROM php:8.3-fpm-alpine AS runtime
COPY . /var/www/html
COPY --from=vendor /app/vendor /var/www/html/vendor
COPY --from=assets /public/build /var/www/html/public/build
36.15 Non-Root Containers Middle

Default holatda konteyner jarayoni root sifatida ishlaydi — konteynerdan qochish zaifligida host'ga root huquq. Yechim: USER www-data (yoki maxsus UID), kerakli papkalarga (storage/, bootstrap/cache/) ruxsat, 1024 dan past portlarni tinglamaslik (Nginx 8080 da).

Qo'shimcha: read-only root fayl tizimi (--read-only + yoziladigan tmpfs/volume'lar), --cap-drop=ALL, no-new-privileges. Kubernetes'da runAsNonRoot: true.

RUN chown -R www-data:www-data storage bootstrap/cache
USER www-data
docker run --read-only --tmpfs /tmp --cap-drop=ALL --security-opt no-new-privileges registry/app:sha

Intervyu savollari

  • middle Multi-stage Dockerfile nima va Laravel loyihasida konteyner image hajmini qanday qilib 1GB dan 70MB gacha kamaytirish mumkin?
  • middle Docker konteynerlarida PHP-FPM yoki Queue Worker'larga signallarni to'g'ri uzatish (Graceful Shutdown) nega muhim?
  • middle DevOps: Docker Multi-Stage Build orqali PHP/Laravel production image hajmini qanday qilib 10 barobarga qisqartiramiz?
  • senior DevOps: Kubernetes Health Checks: Liveness Probe va Readiness Probe ning PHP-FPM podlaridagi vazifasi nima?

38. Linux

38.19 Security Basics Middle

Linux server xavfsizligi asoslari: tizimni yangilab turish, SSH qattiqlashtirish (kalit, root yo'q, fail2ban), firewall (faqat 22, 80, 443 ochiq; DB va Redis faqat ichki), minimal paketlar va servislar, har servis o'z foydalanuvchisi ostida, to'g'ri fayl ruxsatlari (.env 640), sirlar fayllarda emas secret manager'da, log'lar va audit (auth.log), avtomatik xavfsizlik yangilanishlari, muntazam backup.

Ochiq portlarni tekshirish: ss -tlnp va tashqaridan nmap.

ss -tlnp
ufw status verbose
chmod 640 /var/www/shared/.env && chown deploy:www-data /var/www/shared/.env

Intervyu savollari

  • middle DevOps: Linux systemd servisi yaratish: Laravel fon workerlarini yoki Websocket serverni qanday boshqaramiz?
  • middle Server sekinlashdi. Birinchi 5 daqiqada qaysi buyruqlar bilan tashxis qo'yasiz?
  • middle SIGTERM va SIGKILL farqi nima? Laravel queue worker'larini deploy paytida qanday to'g'ri to'xtatasiz?

39. Ubuntu Server

39.10 Server Hardening Middle

Qattiqlashtirish checklist'i: SSH (kalit, root yo'q, fail2ban), firewall (minimal portlar), avtomatik xavfsizlik yangilanishlari, keraksiz servislar o'chirilgan (systemctl list-unit-files --state=enabled), servislar alohida foydalanuvchilarda, fayl ruxsatlari (.env 640), Nginx/PHP versiyani yashirish (server_tokens off, expose_php off), kernel parametrlari (sysctl — SYN cookies, ICMP redirect'lar), audit (auditd), CIS Benchmark yoki Lynis bilan tekshirish.

apt install lynis && lynis audit system
systemctl list-unit-files --state=enabled
39.13 Backup Middle

Server backup'i: nima (DB — eng muhim, foydalanuvchi fayllari agar S3'da bo'lmasa, .env/sirlar — xavfsiz joyda, konfiguratsiya — yaxshisi IaC'da), qanchalik tez-tez (RPO), qayerga (boshqa server/region, S3 — 3-2-1 qoidasi: 3 nusxa, 2 xil vosita, 1 tashqi joyda), shifrlash, saqlash muddati, va eng muhimi — restore testi.

Vositalar: mysqldump/pg_dump + cron, spatie/laravel-backup (DB + fayllar → S3, bildirishnoma, tozalash), restic/borg, provayder snapshot'lari.

// spatie/laravel-backup
Schedule::command('backup:clean')->daily()->at('01:00');
Schedule::command('backup:run --only-db')->hourly();
Schedule::command('backup:monitor')->daily()->at('08:00');

40. Bash

40.15 Error Handling Middle

Bash default holatda xatoni e'tiborsiz qoldirib davom etadi — deploy skriptida bu falokat (migratsiya yiqildi, lekin symlink almashtirildi). Usullar: set -euo pipefail (40.16), har kritik buyruqdan keyin tekshiruv, trap 'cleanup' EXIT (har qanday chiqishda tozalash — lock, vaqtinchalik fayllar), trap 'on_error $LINENO' ERR (xato qatorini log qilish).

Idempotentlik va rollback: skript o'rtada to'xtasa, qayta ishga tushirish xavfsiz bo'lishi; muvaffaqiyatsizlikda oldingi relizga qaytish.

TMP=$(mktemp -d)
cleanup() { rm -rf "$TMP"; }
on_error() { echo "Xato qator $1 da. Rollback..." >&2; ln -sfn "$PREV_RELEASE" /var/www/current; }
trap cleanup EXIT
trap 'on_error $LINENO' ERR
40.16 `set -euo pipefail` Middle

"Strict mode": -e — har qanday buyruq xato bilan tugasa skript to'xtaydi; -u — e'lon qilinmagan o'zgaruvchiga murojaat xato ($TYPO jim bo'sh string bo'lmaydi); -o pipefail — pipe'dagi har qanday buyruq xatosi butun pipe xatosi. Qo'shimcha: IFS=$'\n\t', debug uchun -x.

Nozikliklar: -e if, &&/|| va ! ichidagi buyruqlarga ta'sir qilmaydi; kutilgan xatolar uchun cmd || true; -u bilan ixtiyoriy argumentlar ${1:-}. Har yangi skript shu qator bilan boshlansin.

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
IFS=$'\n\t'

count=$(grep -c ERROR app.log || true)   # 0 natija xato emas
NAME="${1:-default}"

Intervyu savollari

  • senior Deploy skriptida set -euo pipefail nima uchun kerak? Usiz qanday xavf bor?

41. Nginx

41.6 FastCGI Middle

FastCGI — Nginx va PHP-FPM o'rtasidagi protokol. Nginx PHP'ni o'zi bajarmaydi — so'rovni fastcgi_pass orqali FPM'ga yuboradi. Asosiy parametrlar fastcgi_params faylida; eng muhimi SCRIPT_FILENAME (qaysi faylni bajarish) — $realpath_root symlink'li deploy'da to'g'ri reliz yo'lini beradi (OPcache bilan birga).

Bufferlar va timeout'lar: fastcgi_read_timeout (uzoq so'rovlar uchun), fastcgi_buffers/fastcgi_buffer_size (katta header'lar — "upstream sent too big header" xatosi). fastcgi_cache — PHP javoblarini keshlash.

location = /index.php {
    fastcgi_pass unix:/run/php/php8.3-fpm.sock;
    fastcgi_param SCRIPT_FILENAME $realpath_root$fastcgi_script_name;
    fastcgi_param DOCUMENT_ROOT $realpath_root;
    include fastcgi_params;
    fastcgi_read_timeout 60s;
    fastcgi_buffer_size 32k;
    fastcgi_buffers 8 32k;
}
41.7 PHP-FPM Integration Middle

Nginx ↔ PHP-FPM ulanishi: Unix socket (unix:/run/php/php8.3-fpm.sock — bitta serverda tezroq, ruxsatlar kerak) yoki TCP (127.0.0.1:9000 yoki app:9000 — konteynerlar orasida). Socket ruxsatlari: FPM pool listen.owner/listen.group Nginx foydalanuvchisiga mos bo'lishi kerak — aks holda 502.

Bir nechta pool (masalan, admin va API uchun alohida resurslar) — turli socket'lar. FPM status sahifasini faqat ichki manzillarga ochish.

location ~ ^/(fpm-status|fpm-ping)$ {
    allow 127.0.0.1; deny all;
    fastcgi_pass unix:/run/php/php8.3-fpm.sock;
    include fastcgi_params;
    fastcgi_param SCRIPT_FILENAME $fastcgi_script_name;
}
41.9 TLS/HTTPS Middle

TLS sozlash: sertifikat (Let's Encrypt — certbot --nginx, avtomatik yangilanish), listen 443 ssl + http2 on, zamonaviy protokollar (ssl_protocols TLSv1.2 TLSv1.3), xavfsiz shifrlar (Mozilla SSL Configuration Generator), OCSP stapling, session kesh, HTTP → HTTPS 301 yo'naltirish, HSTS header.

Tekshirish: SSL Labs testi (A+ maqsad), openssl s_client, sertifikat muddati monitoringi.

server {
    listen 80;
    server_name app.example.uz;
    return 301 https://$host$request_uri;
}
server {
    listen 443 ssl;
    http2 on;
    ssl_certificate     /etc/letsencrypt/live/app.example.uz/fullchain.pem;
    ssl_certificate_key /etc/letsencrypt/live/app.example.uz/privkey.pem;
    ssl_protocols TLSv1.2 TLSv1.3;
    add_header Strict-Transport-Security "max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains" always;
}
41.18 Security Headers Middle

Xavfsizlik header'lari: Strict-Transport-Security (HTTPS majburiy), X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff, X-Frame-Options: DENY yoki CSP frame-ancestors (clickjacking), Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin, Permissions-Policy (kamera, geolokatsiya), Content-Security-Policy (XSS'ga qarshi kuchli qatlam — ehtiyotkorlik bilan, avval Report-Only).

Nginx'da add_header ... always bilan; meros tuzog'iga e'tibor (41.10). securityheaders.com bilan tekshirish.

# snippets/security-headers.conf
add_header Strict-Transport-Security "max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains" always;
add_header X-Content-Type-Options "nosniff" always;
add_header X-Frame-Options "DENY" always;
add_header Referrer-Policy "strict-origin-when-cross-origin" always;
add_header Content-Security-Policy-Report-Only "default-src 'self'" always;
41.21 Troubleshooting 502/504 Errors Middle

502 Bad Gateway — Nginx backend'dan yaroqli javob ololmadi: FPM ishlamayapti, socket yo'li/ruxsati noto'g'ri, FPM worker crash bo'ldi (segfault, memory_limit), "upstream sent too big header". 504 Gateway Timeout — backend javobi *_read_timeout ichida kelmadi: sekin so'rov, DB lock, tashqi API kutish, FPM worker'lari tugagan (barcha band — navbat).

Diagnostika: Nginx error.log (aniq sabab yoziladi), FPM log va slow log, pm.max_children reached ogohlantirishi, FPM status sahifasi (active processes, listen queue), systemctl status php8.3-fpm.

tail -50 /var/log/nginx/error.log | grep -E "upstream|connect\(\)"
grep -i "max_children" /var/log/php8.3-fpm.log
curl -s http://127.0.0.1/fpm-status?full | head -20

Intervyu savollari

  • senior Nginx FastCGI Buffer Tuning: fastcgi_buffers to'g'ri sozlanmasa, katta javoblarda 'upstream sent too big header' xatosi nima sababdan chiqadi?
  • senior DevOps: Nginx Buffer Overflow va client_body_buffer_size: Katta so'rovlar nega sekinlashadi?
  • senior Saytda vaqti-vaqti bilan 502 va 504 xatolari chiqyapti. Nginx + PHP-FPM muhitida qanday tashxis qo'yasiz?

42. PHP-FPM

42.4 `pm.max_children` Middle

pm.max_children — pool'dagi maksimal worker soni = maksimal parallel PHP so'rovlar. Hisoblash: (Umumiy RAM − boshqa servislar (MySQL, Redis, OS) − zaxira) / o'rtacha worker xotirasi. Masalan: 8 GB, boshqalar 2 GB, zaxira 1 GB, worker 70 MB → ~70.

Juda kichik — so'rovlar navbatda kutadi (504, "max_children reached" log'da); juda katta — xotira tugaydi, swap, OOM killer. DB ulanishlari ham shu songa bog'liq (har worker o'z ulanishi).

pm.max_children = 70
grep "max_children" /var/log/php8.3-fpm.log    # "server reached pm.max_children setting"
42.9 Slow Logs Middle

FPM slow log — request_slowlog_timeout dan uzoq davom etgan so'rovlar uchun PHP stack trace'ni yozadi: qaysi funksiya, qaysi qatorda "qotib qolgan" (DB so'rovi, curl_exec, sleep, fayl I/O). Production'da sekinlik sabablarini topishning eng arzon vositasi.

Odatiy qiymat 2–5 soniya. Log'ni muntazam tahlil qiling: eng ko'p uchraydigan stack'lar — optimallashtirish nomzodlari. APM (Sentry, New Relic) bilan birga to'liqroq rasm.

request_slowlog_timeout = 3s
slowlog = /var/log/php8.3-fpm-slow.log
grep -A3 "script_filename" /var/log/php8.3-fpm-slow.log | grep -oE "\w+\(\) /var/www[^:]+:[0-9]+" | sort | uniq -c | sort -rn | head
42.15 OPcache Middle

OPcache FPM uchun eng muhim performance sozlamasi: kompilyatsiya qilingan opcode'lar shared memory'da barcha worker'lar uchun umumiy. Production: opcache.enable=1, opcache.memory_consumption (Laravel uchun 128–256 MB), opcache.max_accelerated_files (fayllar sonidan katta — vendor bilan 20000+), opcache.interned_strings_buffer (16–32), opcache.validate_timestamps=0 (fayl o'zgarishini tekshirmaslik — deploy'dan keyin FPM reload shart!).

opcache.preload — framework class'larini oldindan yuklash (kichik qo'shimcha foyda). OPcache to'lganini status orqali kuzating (opcache_get_status() — cache_full, hit rate).

opcache.enable=1
opcache.memory_consumption=256
opcache.interned_strings_buffer=32
opcache.max_accelerated_files=30000
opcache.validate_timestamps=0
42.16 Nginx Integration Middle

Nginx ↔ FPM integratsiya checklist'i: fastcgi_pass socket/port FPM listen bilan mos, socket ruxsatlari, SCRIPT_FILENAME = $realpath_root$fastcgi_script_name, faqat index.php bajariladi, timeout'lar muvofiq (Nginx fastcgi_read_timeout ≈ FPM request_terminate_timeout), bufferlar yetarli, status sahifasi faqat ichki.

Symlink deploy'da: $realpath_root + OPcache reload — aks holda eski reliz kodi bajarilishi mumkin. Konteynerlarda: Nginx va FPM bir xil kod yo'liga ega bo'lishi (yoki faqat FPM'da kod, Nginx'da statik fayllar).

location = /index.php {
    fastcgi_pass unix:/run/php/php8.3-fpm.sock;
    fastcgi_param SCRIPT_FILENAME $realpath_root$fastcgi_script_name;
    include fastcgi_params;
    fastcgi_read_timeout 60s;
}

Intervyu savollari

  • middle PHP-FPM arxitekturasida pm.max_children qanday hisoblanadi va noto'g'ri qiymat qanday oqibatlarga olib keladi?
  • senior PHP FPM: pm sozlamasidagi 'static', 'dynamic' va 'ondemand' rejimlarining farqi va ularni server RAM ga qarab qanday sozlash kerak?
  • senior 8 GB RAM'li serverda pm.max_children qiymatini qanday hisoblaysiz?
  • middle Production'da OPcache qanday sozlanishi kerak va deploy'dan keyin eski kod ishlashda davom etsa nima qilasiz?
  • middle PHP-FPM uchun Unix socket va TCP (127.0.0.1:9000) o'rtasidagi farq nima? Qachon qaysi biri?

CI/CD

43. CI/CD

43.8 Environment Separation Middle

Muhitlar: local → CI → staging (production'ga imkon qadar o'xshash) → production. Har biri o'z konfiguratsiyasi (env), sirlari, DB'si va infratuzilmasi bilan; muhitlar o'rtasida ma'lumot va sirlar aralashmaydi (staging production DB'siga ulanmaydi!).

Konfiguratsiya koddan ajratilgan (12-factor) — bir xil artefakt turli env bilan. Staging ma'lumotlari — anonimlashtirilgan yoki sintetik. Preview/review muhitlari — har PR uchun vaqtinchalik.

local       .env (docker)          — dasturchi
staging     secrets: STAGING_*     — QA, demo
production  secrets: PROD_*        — foydalanuvchilar, cheklangan kirish
43.9 Secrets Management Middle

CI/CD'dagi sirlar: deploy kalitlari, registry token'lari, API kalitlari, .env qiymatlari. Qoidalar: hech qachon repozitoriyda emas; CI secret saqlash joyida (masked, protected, environment scope bilan); minimal huquqli va muddatli; log'larga chiqmasin (set -x ehtiyot); bulut uchun uzoq muddatli kalitlar o'rniga OIDC; muntazam aylantirish.

Production .env ni deploy paytida secret manager'dan (Vault, AWS Secrets Manager, Doppler) generatsiya qilish yoki Laravel env:encrypt (shifrlangan .env.production.encrypted + kalit CI'da).

php artisan env:encrypt --env=production   # .env.production.encrypted commit qilinadi
php artisan env:decrypt --env=production --key="$LARAVEL_ENV_ENCRYPTION_KEY"   # deploy'da
43.10 Database Migration Strategy Senior

Deploy'dagi migratsiya — eng xavfli qadam: qaytarib bo'lmaydigan o'zgarishlar, uzoq lock'lar, eski va yangi kod bir vaqtda ishlashi. Strategiya: migratsiyalar orqaga mos (expand/contract — 14.17), deploy'dan oldin alohida bosqichda (migrate --force), bir nechta serverda faqat bir marta (--isolated — Laravel lock bilan), katta jadvallar uchun onlayn migratsiya vositalari, down() ga tayanmaslik (oldinga tuzatish — roll forward).

Migratsiya muvaffaqiyatsiz bo'lsa — deploy to'xtaydi, kod almashtirilmaydi.

php artisan migrate --force --isolated
php artisan migrate --pretend   # PR'da qaysi SQL bajarilishini ko'rsatish
43.12 Rollback Strategy Middle

Har deploy oldindan rejalashtirilgan qaytish yo'liga ega bo'lishi kerak. Usullar: oldingi artefakt/relizga qaytish (symlink almashtirish, oldingi image tag), blue-green'da trafikni qaytarish, feature flag'ni o'chirish (eng tez — kod deploy qilmasdan), roll forward (tez tuzatish deploy'i).

Ma'lumot muammosi: kodni qaytarish oson, DB migratsiyasini qaytarish qiyin — shuning uchun orqaga mos migratsiyalar shart (eski kod yangi sxema bilan ishlay olishi kerak). Rollback'ni muntazam sinab ko'ring — shoshilinch vaqtda birinchi marta qilmaslik uchun.

# releases + symlink
ln -sfn /var/www/releases/20250601120000 /var/www/current && sudo systemctl reload php8.3-fpm
# Kubernetes
kubectl rollout undo deployment/app
43.15 Zero-Downtime Deployment Middle

Zero-downtime — deploy paytida foydalanuvchilar xato yoki uzilishni sezmaydi. Laravel uchun: releases papkalari + atomik symlink almashtirish (Deployer/Envoyer) yoki konteynerlarda rolling update; $realpath_root + FPM graceful reload (OPcache); orqaga mos migratsiyalar (expand/contract); php artisan queue:restart (worker'lar joriy job'ni tugatib yangi kodga o'tadi); health check'dan keyin trafik; asset'lar uchun hash'li nomlar (eski sahifalar eski asset'larni topa olishi).

php artisan down — zero-downtime emas; faqat juda katta, mos bo'lmagan migratsiyalar uchun oxirgi chora.

1. releases/NEW ga kod + composer + build
2. shared: .env, storage symlink
3. migrate --force --isolated (orqaga mos)
4. optimize → ln -sfn NEW current → reload FPM → queue:restart
5. health check ✅ → eski relizlarni tozalash

Intervyu savollari

  • lead Canary Releases va Feature Flags (Laravel Pennant): Yangi funksiyani productionga nol xavf bilan qanday chiqaramiz?
  • senior DevOps: Zero-Downtime Deployment strategiyalari: Blue-Green vs Canary vs Rolling Updates?
  • lead DevOps: Queue Autoscaling: KEDA (Kubernetes) orqali navbat o'lchamiga qarab workerlar qanday avtomat ko'payadi?

44. GitLab CI/CD

44.4 `rules` va `workflow` Middle

rules: — job qachon pipeline'ga qo'shilishini belgilaydi (eskirgan only/except o'rniga): if (o'zgaruvchilar — $CI_PIPELINE_SOURCE == "merge_request_event", $CI_COMMIT_BRANCH, $CI_COMMIT_TAG), changes (faqat ma'lum fayllar o'zgarganda), exists, when (on_success, manual, never, delayed), allow_failure. Birinchi mos qoida qo'llanadi.

workflow: rules — butun pipeline yaratilishini boshqaradi: dublikat pipeline'larning oldini olish (branch va MR uchun ikki marta ishlamasligi).

workflow:
  rules:
    - if: $CI_PIPELINE_SOURCE == "merge_request_event"
    - if: $CI_COMMIT_BRANCH && $CI_OPEN_MERGE_REQUESTS
      when: never
    - if: $CI_COMMIT_BRANCH == $CI_DEFAULT_BRANCH
    - if: $CI_COMMIT_TAG
frontend:
  rules:
    - changes: [resources/js/**/*, package*.json]
44.8 Services Middle

services: — job davomida yonida ishlaydigan qo'shimcha konteynerlar: MySQL, PostgreSQL, Redis, Meilisearch. Ular alias (hostname) orqali topiladi (mysql, redis); konfiguratsiya variables orqali. Testlar uchun haqiqiy DB — production engine'iga mos.

Nozikliklar: servis tayyor bo'lishini kutish (health check yo'q — skriptda kutish), versiyalarni production bilan moslash, xotira (tmpfs bilan tez DB — custom command), servis log'lari (CI_DEBUG_SERVICES).

pest:
  services:
    - name: mysql:8.4
      alias: mysql
      command: ["--innodb-flush-log-at-trx-commit=2"]
    - redis:7
  variables:
    DB_HOST: mysql
    REDIS_HOST: redis
  script:
    - until mysqladmin ping -h mysql --silent; do sleep 1; done
    - php artisan test --parallel
44.10 Test Pipelines Middle

Laravel test pipeline'i: composer (kesh + artefakt) → parallel: pint, phpstan, pest (servislar bilan, shard'lar), composer audit, ixtiyoriy npm run lint/vitest. Hisobotlar: JUnit (MR'da yiqilgan testlar), coverage (coverage: '/^\s*Lines:\s*\d+.\d+\%/' regex — MR'da foiz), Code Quality (PHPStan JSON → codequality format).

MR pipeline'lari tez bo'lishi kerak (10 daqiqadan kam ideal) — kesh, parallellik va changes qoidalari bilan.

pest:
  stage: test
  script: php artisan test --parallel --coverage-text --log-junit=junit.xml
  coverage: '/^\s*Lines:\s*(\d+\.\d+)%/'
  artifacts:
    when: always
    reports: { junit: junit.xml }
44.13 Protected Variables Middle

Protected o'zgaruvchilar faqat protected branch va tag'larda ishlaydigan pipeline'larga beriladi — feature branch'dagi (ehtimoliy zararli) kod production sirlarini o'qiy olmaydi. Masked — log'da [MASKED]. Environment scope — production deploy job'iga faqat production qiymatlari.

Kombinatsiya: production sirlari = protected + masked + environment scope production. "File" turi — kalitlar va sertifikatlar uchun. Muntazam aylantirish va kirish huquqlari (faqat Maintainer'lar ko'radi).

deploy_production:
  environment: production
  script:
    - ssh -i "$DEPLOY_SSH_KEY" deploy@$PROD_HOST "./deploy.sh $CI_COMMIT_SHA"   # DEPLOY_SSH_KEY — File, protected
44.16 Merge Request Pipelines Middle

MR pipeline'lari ($CI_PIPELINE_SOURCE == "merge_request_event") — MR kontekstida ishlaydi: MR widget'ida natijalar, "merge when pipeline succeeds". Merged results pipeline — MR branch'i maqsad branch bilan birlashtirilgan holatni sinaydi (birlashtirgandan keyingi buzilishlarni oldindan topadi). Merge train — bir nechta MR navbat bilan ketma-ket birlashtirilgan holatda sinaladi (main hech qachon qizil bo'lmaydi).

workflow:rules bilan MR va branch pipeline'larini to'g'ri ajratish (dublikatlarsiz).

workflow:
  rules:
    - if: $CI_PIPELINE_SOURCE == "merge_request_event"
    - if: $CI_COMMIT_BRANCH == $CI_DEFAULT_BRANCH

Intervyu savollari

  • middle GitLab CI'da cache va artifacts farqi nima? Laravel pipeline'ida nimani qayerda saqlaysiz?
  • senior GitLab CI'da production deploy sirlarini qanday himoya qilasiz?

45. GitHub Actions

45.7 Secrets va Variables Middle

Secret'lar (repo, environment, organization darajasida) — ${{ secrets.NAME }}, log'da niqoblanadi; variables (${{ vars.NAME }}) — maxfiy bo'lmagan konfiguratsiya. Environment secret'lari faqat shu environment'dagi job'larga. Fork PR'larida secret'lar yo'q (GITHUB_TOKEN faqat o'qish).

Ehtiyot: secret'ni echo qilmaslik, artefaktga yozmaslik, strukturali sirlar (JSON) har qismini alohida niqoblamasligi mumkin (::add-mask::), secret'larni shell buyrug'iga to'g'ridan-to'g'ri interpolatsiya qilish o'rniga env: orqali uzatish (injection'ga qarshi).

- name: Deploy
  env:
    SSH_KEY: ${{ secrets.DEPLOY_SSH_KEY }}
    HOST: ${{ vars.PROD_HOST }}
  run: ./deploy.sh      # skript $SSH_KEY va $HOST ni env'dan o'qiydi
45.13 Permissions Middle

GITHUB_TOKEN — har workflow uchun avtomatik token; uning ruxsatlari permissions: bilan workflow yoki job darajasida belgilanadi. Eng kam huquq: default contents: read, kerakli joyda qo'shimcha (pull-requests: write — PR izohi uchun, packages: write — ghcr push, id-token: write — OIDC, deployments: write).

Repozitoriy/tashkilot sozlamasida default ruxsatni "read-only" ga qo'yish tavsiya etiladi. Boshqa repozitoriylarga kirish uchun — GitHub App token (actions/create-github-app-token), shaxsiy PAT emas.

permissions:
  contents: read
jobs:
  publish:
    permissions:
      contents: read
      packages: write
45.16 CI/CD Security Senior

GitHub Actions xavfsizlik checklist'i: minimal permissions; SHA pinning; pull_request_target va workflow_run da ishonchsiz kodni checkout qilmaslik; script injection — ${{ github.event.pull_request.title }} kabi foydalanuvchi ma'lumotini run: ga to'g'ridan-to'g'ri qo'ymaslik (env orqali); secret'lar environment'larda; OIDC; self-hosted runner'larni ommaviy repo'larda ishlatmaslik; workflow fayllari o'zgarishiga CODEOWNERS; zizmor/actionlint bilan statik tahlil.

# ❌ injection: PR sarlavhasi shell buyrug'iga aylanadi
- run: echo "${{ github.event.pull_request.title }}"
# ✅
- env: { TITLE: '${{ github.event.pull_request.title }}' }
  run: echo "$TITLE"

  • senior GitHub Actions'dan AWS'ga deploy qilishda uzoq muddatli access key o'rniga OIDC qanday ishlaydi?
  • middle Uchinchi tomon GitHub Action'larini ishlatishda qanday xavflar bor va ulardan qanday himoyalanasiz?

Navbatlar va hodisalar

46. Queues & Background Jobs

46.6 Retries va Backoff Middle

Qayta urinishlar: $tries (urinishlar soni), retryUntil() (vaqt bo'yicha chegara — tashqi API uchun qulay), $maxExceptions (shuncha exception'dan keyin to'xtash, release() lar hisobga olinmaydi), $backoff — son yoki massiv (eksponensial: [10, 60, 300]). Qo'lda: $this->release(30) (qayta navbatga), $this->fail($e) (darhol muvaffaqiyatsiz — qayta urinish foydasiz bo'lsa, masalan, validatsiya xatosi).

Qayta urinish faqat vaqtinchalik xatolar uchun ma'noli (timeout, 503, deadlock). Doimiy xatolarni (404, noto'g'ri ma'lumot) darhol fail() qiling.

public $tries = 5;
public $maxExceptions = 3;
public function backoff(): array { return [10, 60, 300, 900]; }

public function handle(Gateway $gw): void
{
    try { $gw->charge($this->payment); }
    catch (CardDeclined $e) { $this->fail($e); }   // qayta urinish ma'nosiz
}
46.7 Timeout Configuration Middle

Job timeout: $timeout (soniya — oshsa worker jarayoni o'ldiriladi, pcntl kengaytmasi kerak), $failOnTimeout = true (timeout'da qayta urinmasdan muvaffaqiyatsiz). Worker darajasida --timeout. Connection'da retry_after — job shu vaqt ichida tugamasa boshqa worker uni qayta oladi.

Oltin qoida: retry_after > eng uzun job timeout (bir necha soniya zaxira bilan); aks holda job ikki marta parallel bajariladi. Horizon'da supervisor timeout ham shu qoidaga bo'ysunadi. Uzoq job'larni bo'laklarga bo'lish yoki batch.

final class ImportCatalog implements ShouldQueue
{
    public $timeout = 600;
    public $failOnTimeout = true;
}
// config/queue.php → 'retry_after' => 660
46.8 Failed Jobs Middle

Muvaffaqiyatsiz job'lar failed_jobs jadvalida (UUID, connection, queue, payload, exception, vaqt). Boshqaruv: queue:failed (ro'yxat), queue:retry {uuid|all}, queue:retry --queue=emails, queue:forget, queue:flush, queue:prune-failed --hours=168. Job'dagi failed(Throwable $e) metodi — tozalash, foydalanuvchiga xabar, kompensatsiya.

Muvaffaqiyatsiz job'lar — monitoring va alert manbai; ularni tahlil qilmasdan ommaviy retry all — xatoni takrorlashi mumkin. DynamoDB yoki boshqa saqlash ham mumkin (failed.driver).

public function failed(Throwable $e): void
{
    $this->export->update(['status' => 'failed', 'error' => Str::limit($e->getMessage(), 500)]);
    $this->export->user->notify(new ExportFailed($this->export));
}
46.14 Idempotent Job Design Senior

Navbatlar at-least-once — job ikki marta bajarilishi mumkin (worker crash, retry_after, qayta urinish, qo'lda retry). Idempotent job — takror bajarilganda natija o'zgarmaydi. Usullar: holat tekshiruvi ("allaqachon yuborilganmi?"), unique constraint (processed_job_keys), upsert, idempotency key bilan tashqi API chaqiruvi, yon ta'sirlarni oxirida va bir martalik belgilab bajarish.

Qisman bajarilish: job o'rtada yiqilsa, qayta urinishda boshidan ishlaydi — har qadam qayta bajarishga xavfsiz bo'lsin.

public function handle(Mailer $mailer): void
{
    $sent = InvoiceDelivery::firstOrCreate(['invoice_id' => $this->invoice->id], ['status' => 'pending']);
    if ($sent->status === 'sent') {
        return;
    }
    $mailer->to($this->invoice->customer)->send(new InvoiceMail($this->invoice));
    $sent->update(['status' => 'sent']);
}
46.16 Queue Workers Middle

php artisan queue:work — long-running jarayon: bir marta bootstrap, job'larni siklda oladi (tez). Opsiyalar: --queue, --tries, --timeout, --sleep (bo'sh navbatda kutish), --max-jobs, --max-time (davriy qayta ishga tushirish), --memory, --stop-when-empty, --backoff. queue:listen — har job'da qayta bootstrap (sekin, faqat dev).

Worker'lar jarayon menejeri ostida (Supervisor, systemd, Horizon, konteyner) — crash'da qayta ishga tushirish. Kod o'zgargach eski kodda ishlaydi — deploy'da qayta ishga tushirish shart.

[program:worker]
command=php /var/www/current/artisan queue:work redis --queue=default --sleep=3 --tries=3 --max-time=3600 --memory=256
numprocs=4
autorestart=true
stopwaitsecs=360
46.18 Graceful Worker Restart Middle

Deploy'dan keyin worker'lar eski kodni ishlatadi (bootstrap bir marta). php artisan queue:restart — cache'ga signal yozadi; har worker joriy job'ini tugatgach to'xtaydi va jarayon menejeri uni yangi kod bilan qayta ishga tushiradi. Horizon: horizon:terminate.

Shartlar: cache barcha serverlar uchun umumiy (Redis), jarayon menejerining stopwaitsecs eng uzun job'dan katta, SIGTERM (SIGKILL emas) — joriy job yarim qolmasligi uchun. Payload formati o'zgargan bo'lsa — eski job'lar bilan moslik.

# deploy skriptida
php artisan queue:restart       # yoki: php artisan horizon:terminate

Intervyu savollari

  • middle Laravel Navbatlarida (Queues) Idempotent Job tushunchasi nima va tarmoq xatolarida takroriy ijrodan qanday himoyalanamiz?
  • middle Dead Letter Queue (DLQ) nima va qayta-qayta xato beruvchi zaharli xabarlar (Poison Messages) qanday boshqariladi?
  • senior Incident Triage: Laravel Queue Worker'lari Out of Memory (OOM) bo'lib to'xtab qolyapti. Sababi nima va qanday bartaraf etiladi?
  • middle Laravel Queues: ShouldBeUnique va ShouldBeUniqueUntilProcessing interfeyslarining nozik farqi nima?
  • middle Queues: Delayed Jobs (Kechiktirilgan vazifalar) va Scheduled Tasks kapot ostida qanday ishlaydi?
  • senior Queues: Poison Pill nima va Dead Letter Queue (DLQ) dagi xatolarni avtomat qayta ishlash qanday yo'lga qo'yiladi?
  • middle Laravel Queues: Job Retries, backoff, tries va maxExceptions parametrlari tarmoq xatolarini qanday yengadi?
  • middle Linux Supervisor: Production muhitida Laravel queue workerlarni doimiy va barqaror tirik saqlash qanday ta'minlanadi?
  • senior Bir kechada navbatda 200 000 ta job to'planib qoldi. Qanday harakat qilasiz?

47. Laravel Horizon

47.2 Redis Queue Configuration Middle

Horizon uchun Redis sozlamalari: alohida Redis connection (queue) va ideal holda alohida instance yoki DB (cache bilan aralashmasin — noeviction siyosati!), retry_after > eng uzun timeout, block_for (BLPOP kutish — resurs tejash), prefiks (horizon.prefix) — bir Redis'da bir nechta ilova.

Redis persistence (AOF) — restart'da navbatdagi job'lar yo'qolmasligi uchun. Cluster'da hash tag'lar bilan prefiks.

// config/queue.php
'redis' => [
    'driver' => 'redis',
    'connection' => 'queue',
    'queue' => 'default',
    'retry_after' => 190,
    'block_for' => 5,
],
// config/database.php → 'redis' → 'queue' => [... 'database' => 2]
47.3 Supervisors Middle

Supervisor — navbatlar guruhi uchun worker'lar to'plami: connection, queue (ro'yxat — tartib prioritetni bildiradi), balance, minProcesses/maxProcesses, tries, timeout, memory, nice. Muhit bo'yicha (environments.production, local) turli sozlamalar.

Odatiy amaliyot: navbatlarni xususiyatiga qarab supervisor'larga ajratish — tez va muhim (critical, default), sekin og'ir (imports, reports — kattaroq timeout va memory), tashqi API (webhooks — ko'p parallel, kichik xotira). Shunda og'ir job'lar tezlarini bloklamaydi.

'environments' => [
    'production' => [
        'supervisor-fast' => ['queue' => ['critical', 'default'], 'balance' => 'auto', 'minProcesses' => 2, 'maxProcesses' => 20, 'timeout' => 60],
        'supervisor-heavy' => ['queue' => ['imports', 'reports'], 'balance' => 'simple', 'maxProcesses' => 4, 'timeout' => 900, 'memory' => 512],
    ],
],
47.8 Timeouts Middle

Horizon'da timeout'lar: supervisor timeout (worker job'ni shu soniyadan keyin o'ldiradi), job $timeout (ustunlik qiladi), connection retry_after. Qoida: retry_after > eng katta timeout. Horizon horizon:terminate dagi kutish — supervisor timeout'lariga bog'liq.

Turli timeout'li job'lar — turli supervisor'larda (uzun job'lar tez navbatni egallab qolmasligi va retry_after qoidasi buzilmasligi uchun alohida connection bilan).

'connections' => [
    'redis'      => [..., 'retry_after' => 90],
    'redis-long' => [..., 'retry_after' => 960],
],
'supervisor-heavy' => ['connection' => 'redis-long', 'queue' => ['imports'], 'timeout' => 900],
47.14 Dashboard Access Control Middle

Horizon dashboard'i job payload'larini (shaxsiy ma'lumot bo'lishi mumkin), xatolarni ko'rsatadi va job'larni qayta ishga tushirishga imkon beradi — production'da qat'iy himoya kerak. HorizonServiceProvider::gate() — viewHorizon gate'i (default: faqat local muhitda ochiq).

Qo'shimcha: dashboard yo'lini o'zgartirish (path), middleware (auth, IP cheklovi, VPN), admin rollar, sezgir job'larni ShouldBeEncrypted bilan shifrlash (payload dashboard'da shifrlangan ko'rinadi).

protected function gate(): void
{
    Gate::define('viewHorizon', fn (User $user) => $user->hasRole('devops') || $user->hasRole('admin'));
}
// config/horizon.php
'middleware' => ['web', 'auth', 'verified'],
47.16 Graceful Restarts Middle

horizon:terminate — graceful: yangi job'larni olishni to'xtatadi, joriy job'larni tugatadi, keyin chiqadi. horizon:pause / horizon:continue — vaqtincha to'xtatish (masalan, DB texnik xizmati paytida); horizon:pause-supervisor — faqat bitta supervisor. horizon:status.

SIGTERM (graceful) va SIGKILL (darhol) farqi: jarayon menejeri stopwaitsecs kutib, keyin SIGKILL yuboradi — shuning uchun bu qiymat yetarli bo'lishi kerak, aks holda uzun job yarim qoladi (idempotentlik muhimligi yana bir bor).

php artisan horizon:pause          # texnik xizmatdan oldin
php artisan horizon:continue
php artisan horizon:terminate      # deploy

Intervyu savollari

  • senior Laravel Horizon'da auto-balancing strategiyasi qanday ishlaydi va Queue Worker'larda Memory Leak qanday oldini olinadi?
  • senior Queues: Laravel Horizon'da Backpressure nima va so'rovlar oqimi workerlar tezligidan oshib ketganda tizim qanday himoyalanadi?

48. Event-Driven Architecture

48.6 Duplicate Event Handling Senior

At-least-once yetkazish → dublikatlar muqarrar (broker retry, producer qayta yuborishi, iste'molchi ACK'dan oldin crash). Iste'molchi har event'ni event_id bo'yicha deduplikatsiya qiladi: "processed events" jadvali (unique constraint) yoki Redis set (TTL bilan), biznes o'zgarishi bilan bir tranzaksiyada.

Deduplikatsiya oynasi — broker retry muddatidan uzun. Producer tomonida ham idempotentlik (outbox bitta xabarni ikki marta yubormasligi uchun relay holati).

DB::transaction(function () use ($event) {
    if (! ProcessedEvent::insertOrIgnore(['event_id' => $event->id, 'consumer' => 'billing'])) {
        return; // allaqachon qayta ishlangan
    }
    $this->handleBusinessLogic($event);
});
48.7 Idempotent Consumers Senior

Idempotent iste'molchi — bir event'ni necha marta qayta ishlasa ham natija bir xil. Usullar: deduplikatsiya (48.6), tabiiy idempotent amallar (SET status = paid — increment emas), upsert, holat tekshiruvi, versiya tekshiruvi. Tashqi yon ta'sirlar (email, to'lov) — idempotency key bilan yoki "yuborildi" belgisi bilan.

EDA'da idempotentlik — ixtiyoriy emas, balki asosiy talab; uni test bilan (bir event'ni ikki marta yuborish) majburiy qiling.

it('applies payment only once', function () {
    $event = new PaymentCaptured(eventId: 'evt_1', orderId: $order->id, amount: 150_000);
    app(ApplyPayment::class)->handle($event);
    app(ApplyPayment::class)->handle($event);
    expect($order->fresh()->paid_amount)->toBe(150_000);
});
48.10 Eventual Consistency Senior

EDA'da ma'lumot turli komponentlarda darhol emas, vaqt o'tib izchil bo'ladi: buyurtma yaratildi, lekin hisobot servisida bir necha soniyadan keyin ko'rinadi. Bu tarmoqli tizimlarning tabiiy holati (CAP), xato emas — lekin biznes va UX bilan kelishilishi kerak.

Boshqarish: foydalanuvchiga oraliq holatni ko'rsatish ("to'lov tekshirilmoqda"), o'z yozganini darhol ko'rish (read-your-writes — natijani lokal qaytarish), moslashtirish (reconciliation) job'lari nomuvofiqliklarni topish uchun, kechikish metrikasi (event yaratilgan va qayta ishlangan vaqt farqi).

T0  Order servisi: order #42 = placed      ✅
T0  Foydalanuvchiga: "Buyurtma qabul qilindi, tasdiqlanmoqda"
T+2s Inventory: zaxira band qilindi → OrderConfirmed
T+3s UI: "Tasdiqlandi"

Intervyu savollari

  • senior RabbitMQ va Apache Kafka o'rtasidagi asosiy farqlar nima? Qachon qaysi biri tanlanadi?
  • lead Event Sourcing va CQRS nima va ular bank/moliya tizimlarida nima uchun oddiy CRUD o'rniga tanlanadi?
  • senior Architecture: Idempotent Consumer Pattern: Navbatdagi (Queue) xabarlar takroran kelganda (At-Least-Once Delivery) dublikatlardan qanday himoyalanamiz?
  • lead Architecture: Event Sourcing nima va nima uchun Snapshotting (Kesimlar) bilan birga qo'llanilishi shart?
  • senior Kafka vs RabbitMQ: Push va Pull arxitekturasi hamda Log-based va Queue-based modellarning tub farqi nima?
  • middle Laravel: Event / Listener arxitekturasi qanday ishlaydi va Event Discovery mexanizmi nima?
  • senior Apache Kafka: Partition Key tanlash strategiyasi va Consumer Rebalance paytida yuzaga keladigan Stop-The-World muammosi?
  • senior Event Sourcing: Event Schema Evolution: Yillar davomida to'plangan millionlab eski hodisalarni yangi kod talablariga moslash (Upcasting) qanday yechiladi?

49. RabbitMQ

49.1 Exchanges Middle

RabbitMQ'da producer xabarni to'g'ridan-to'g'ri navbatga emas, exchange'ga yuboradi; exchange esa qoidalar (binding) bo'yicha uni bir yoki bir nechta navbatga yo'naltiradi. Bu producer'ni iste'molchilar tuzilmasidan ajratadi.

Exchange xususiyatlari: tur (direct, topic, fanout, headers), durable (restart'dan keyin saqlanadi), auto-delete, alternate-exchange (hech qaysi navbatga mos kelmagan xabarlar uchun — yo'qolmasligi uchun). Default exchange ("") — routing key = navbat nomi.

$channel->exchange_declare('orders', 'topic', passive: false, durable: true, auto_delete: false);
$channel->basic_publish(new AMQPMessage($json, ['delivery_mode' => 2]), 'orders', 'order.placed');
49.2 Queues Middle

Navbat — xabarlar saqlanadigan va iste'molchilar o'qiydigan bufer (FIFO). Xususiyatlar: durable, exclusive, auto-delete, argumentlar: x-message-ttl, x-max-length (+ x-overflow), x-dead-letter-exchange, x-queue-type: quorum (replikatsiyalangan, tavsiya etiladi — klassik mirrored o'rniga), lazy rejim.

Bir navbatga bir nechta iste'molchi — xabarlar ular o'rtasida taqsimlanadi (competing consumers — ish taqsimlash). Har iste'molchi turiga alohida navbat (pub/sub — har biri o'z nusxasini oladi).

$channel->queue_declare('billing.order-events', durable: true, arguments: new AMQPTable([
    'x-queue-type' => 'quorum',
    'x-dead-letter-exchange' => 'dlx',
]));
49.3 Bindings Middle

Binding — exchange va navbat (yoki exchange va exchange) o'rtasidagi qoida: "shu routing key/pattern'li xabarlarni shu navbatga". Bitta navbat bir nechta binding'ga ega bo'lishi mumkin; bitta xabar bir nechta navbatga ketishi mumkin.

Binding'lar marshrutlash topologiyasini belgilaydi — uni kod (yoki definitions JSON / IaC) bilan versiyalang, qo'lda UI'da yaratmang. Iste'molchi o'z navbati va binding'larini e'lon qiladi (idempotent declare).

$channel->queue_bind('billing.order-events', 'orders', 'order.placed');
$channel->queue_bind('billing.order-events', 'orders', 'order.cancelled');
$channel->queue_bind('analytics.all-orders', 'orders', 'order.#');
49.4 Routing Keys Middle

Routing key — xabar bilan yuboriladigan string, exchange uni binding key bilan solishtiradi. Topic exchange'da nuqtali ierarxiya: order.placed, order.payment.failed; pattern'lar: * — bitta so'z, # — nol yoki ko'p so'z (order.#, *.failed).

Nomlash konvensiyasi muhim (<domen>.<obyekt>.<hodisa>, kichik harf) — yangi iste'molchilar wildcard bilan kerakli event'larga oson obuna bo'lishi uchun. Routing key'ga ID kabi yuqori kardinallikdagi qiymatlarni qo'ymang.

order.placed           → billing (order.placed), analytics (order.#)
order.payment.failed   → notifications (*.payment.failed), analytics (order.#)
49.5 Direct, Topic, Fanout va Headers Exchanges Middle
  • Direct — routing key binding key bilan aniq teng bo'lsa (vazifalar turini yo'naltirish: pdf, email).
  • Topic — pattern bo'yicha (order.*) — event'lar uchun eng moslashuvchan.
  • Fanout — routing key'ni e'tiborsiz qoldirib, barcha bog'langan navbatlarga (broadcast — kesh invalidatsiyasi, bildirishnomalar).
  • Headers — routing key o'rniga xabar header'lari bo'yicha (x-match: all/any) — kam ishlatiladi.

Tanlov: buyruqlar/vazifalar — direct; domen event'lari — topic; hammaga xabar — fanout.

$channel->exchange_declare('tasks', 'direct', durable: true);
$channel->exchange_declare('cache-invalidation', 'fanout', durable: true);
49.7 Acknowledgements Middle

Consumer ACK — "xabar muvaffaqiyatli qayta ishlandi, o'chirish mumkin". Manual ACK (no_ack: false) — qayta ishlagandan keyin ACK: crash'da xabar boshqa iste'molchiga qayta beriladi (at-least-once). Auto ACK — yetkazilishi bilan o'chiriladi (tez, lekin crash'da yo'qoladi — at-most-once).

nack/reject: requeue: true (navbatga qaytarish — zaharli xabar cheksiz siklga tushishi mumkin!), requeue: false (DLX'ga yoki o'chirish). ACK unutilsa — xabarlar "unacked" holatda qoladi va prefetch to'lib, consumer to'xtaydi.

try {
    $this->process($msg);
    $msg->ack();
} catch (TransientError $e) {
    $msg->nack(requeue: true);    // ehtiyotkorlik: retry hisoblagichi kerak
} catch (Throwable $e) {
    $msg->reject(requeue: false); // DLX
}
49.9 Durable Queues Middle

Durable navbat va exchange — broker restart'dan keyin saqlanadi (metadata). Lekin xabarlarning o'zi saqlanishi uchun ular ham persistent bo'lishi kerak (49.10). Non-durable navbatlar — vaqtinchalik, masalan RPC javoblari yoki exclusive iste'molchi navbatlari.

Yuqori mavjudlik uchun quorum queues (Raft asosida, bir nechta tugunda replikatsiya) — klassik mirrored queues eskirgan. Quorum navbatlarda x-delivery-limit (zaharli xabarlarni avtomatik DLX'ga) mavjud.

$channel->queue_declare('payments', durable: true, arguments: new AMQPTable([
    'x-queue-type' => 'quorum',
    'x-delivery-limit' => 5,
]));
49.10 Persistent Messages Middle

Persistent xabar (delivery_mode = 2) — broker uni diskka yozadi; durable navbatda bo'lsa restart'dan keyin saqlanadi. Ikkalasi ham kerak: durable navbat + persistent xabar. Disk yozuvi throughput'ni pasaytiradi — muhim bo'lmagan xabarlar (metrikalar) uchun transient bo'lishi mumkin.

To'liq kafolat zanjiri: persistent xabar + durable/quorum navbat + publisher confirms + manual consumer ACK. Bittasi yo'q bo'lsa — xabar yo'qolish ehtimoli.

$msg = new AMQPMessage(json_encode($payload), [
    'delivery_mode' => AMQPMessage::DELIVERY_MODE_PERSISTENT,
    'content_type'  => 'application/json',
    'message_id'    => $eventId,
    'timestamp'     => time(),
]);
49.13 Dead Letter Exchanges Middle

DLX — navbat argumenti (x-dead-letter-exchange, ixtiyoriy x-dead-letter-routing-key): xabar reject/nack(requeue=false), TTL tugashi, navbat uzunligi limiti yoki quorum delivery-limit oshishi sababli "o'lganda" shu exchange'ga yuboriladi. x-death header'i — sabab, navbat va son.

DLX'ga bog'langan DLQ — monitoring, tahlil va redrive (shovel yoki skript bilan asosiy exchange'ga qaytarish) uchun. Har asosiy navbat uchun DLQ — standart amaliyot.

$channel->exchange_declare('dlx', 'topic', durable: true);
$channel->queue_declare('billing.order-events.dlq', durable: true);
$channel->queue_bind('billing.order-events.dlq', 'dlx', '#');
49.16 Idempotent Consumers Senior

RabbitMQ at-least-once yetkazadi: consumer ACK'dan oldin crash, tarmoq uzilishi, nack(requeue), producer qayta yuborishi — dublikatlar. Har iste'molchi message_id (yoki payload'dagi event_id) bo'yicha deduplikatsiya qiladi: biznes o'zgarishi bilan bir tranzaksiyada "processed" yozuvi (unique), keyin ACK.

ACK'ni DB commit'dan keyin yuboring: commit muvaffaqiyatli, ACK yo'qoldi → xabar qayta keladi → deduplikatsiya uni o'tkazib yuboradi. Teskari tartib xabar yo'qolishiga olib keladi.

DB::transaction(function () use ($msg, $payload) {
    ProcessedMessage::create(['message_id' => $msg->get('message_id'), 'consumer' => 'billing']);
    $this->apply($payload);
});
$msg->ack();

Intervyu savollari

  • middle RabbitMQ Exchange turlari (Direct, Fanout, Topic, Headers) qanday farqlanadi va qachon qaysi biri tanlanadi?
  • senior Queues: RabbitMQ Exchanges turlari: Direct, Topic, Fanout va Headers qachon qaysi biri ishlatiladi?

Xavfsizlik

50. HTTP/HTTPS

50.8 TLS Handshake Middle

TLS handshake — xavfsiz kanal o'rnatish: mijoz qo'llab-quvvatlaydigan versiya va shifrlarni yuboradi (ClientHello, SNI — qaysi domen), server sertifikat va kalit almashish parametrlarini yuboradi, mijoz sertifikatni tekshiradi, ikkalasi umumiy sessiya kalitini hosil qiladi (ECDHE — forward secrecy). TLS 1.3 — 1 RTT (TLS 1.2'da 2 RTT), eskirgan shifrlar olib tashlangan, 0-RTT resumption.

Kechikishga ta'siri: har yangi ulanishda handshake — keep-alive, session resumption, HTTP/2 (bitta ulanish) uni kamaytiradi.

openssl s_client -connect app.example.uz:443 -servername app.example.uz -tls1_3 </dev/null 2>/dev/null | grep -E "Protocol|Cipher"
curl -w "tls: %{time_appconnect}s\n" -o /dev/null -s https://app.example.uz
50.13 Connection Timeouts Middle

Timeout turlari: connect timeout (ulanish o'rnatish — tarmoq/DNS muammosi, qisqa: 2–5 s), read/response timeout (javobni kutish — server sekinligi), umumiy timeout. Timeout'siz tashqi chaqiruv — eng xavfli xatolardan biri: tashqi servis qotib qolsa, PHP-FPM worker'lari band bo'ladi va butun ilova to'xtaydi.

Laravel HTTP client: ->connectTimeout(3)->timeout(10), ->retry(3, 200, throw: false) (faqat idempotent so'rovlar uchun yoki idempotency key bilan). Timeout'lar zanjiri: mijoz < Nginx < PHP — ichkaridagisi kichikroq.

$response = Http::connectTimeout(3)
    ->timeout(10)
    ->retry(2, 300, fn ($e) => $e instanceof ConnectionException)
    ->get('https://api.provider.uz/rates');
50.14 CORS Middle

CORS (Cross-Origin Resource Sharing) — brauzer xavfsizlik mexanizmi: boshqa origin'dagi (sxema + domen + port) API'ga JS so'rovi faqat server ruxsat bersa javobni o'qishga imkon beradi. Oddiy so'rovlar va preflight (OPTIONS — murakkab so'rovlar: PUT, DELETE, JSON content-type, custom header'lar) — Access-Control-Allow-Origin, -Methods, -Headers, -Credentials, -Max-Age.

CORS server himoyasi emas — u faqat brauzerga ta'sir qiladi (curl yoki server-server so'rovlarni to'xtatmaydi); avtorizatsiya baribir kerak. Credentials (cookie) bilan * ishlatib bo'lmaydi. Laravel: config/cors.php.

OPTIONS /api/orders HTTP/1.1
Origin: https://app.example.uz
Access-Control-Request-Method: PATCH

HTTP/1.1 204 No Content
Access-Control-Allow-Origin: https://app.example.uz
Access-Control-Allow-Methods: GET, POST, PATCH, DELETE
Access-Control-Allow-Credentials: true
Access-Control-Max-Age: 600
50.22 HTTPS Security Middle

HTTPS xavfsizligi: barcha trafik HTTPS (HTTP → 301 yo'naltirish), HSTS (Strict-Transport-Security — brauzer keyinchalik faqat HTTPS ishlatadi; preload ro'yxati), zamonaviy TLS (1.2+, xavfsiz shifrlar, forward secrecy), to'liq sertifikat zanjiri, aralash kontent yo'q (HTTPS sahifada HTTP resurslar), cookie'lar Secure.

Laravel: URL::forceScheme('https') (proxy ortida noto'g'ri sozlangan bo'lsa), TrustProxies (sxemani to'g'ri aniqlash), secure session cookie. Tekshirish: SSL Labs, curl -I bilan HSTS header'i.

// AppServiceProvider::boot()
if ($this->app->isProduction()) {
    URL::forceScheme('https');
}
Strict-Transport-Security: max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains; preload

Intervyu savollari

  • senior Networking: HTTP/2 va HTTP/3 (QUIC) ning HTTP/1.1 dan tub ustunliklari: Multiplexing va Head-of-Line Blocking?

51. Web Application Security

51.8 Server-Side Request Forgery (SSRF) Middle

SSRF — hujumchi serverni o'zi tanlagan manzilga so'rov yuborishga majburlaydi: ichki servislar (localhost:6379, admin panellar), bulut metadata (169.254.169.254 — IAM kalitlari!), ichki tarmoq skanerlash. Manbalar: URL'dan rasm yuklash, webhook URL'lari, PDF generatsiya (HTML'dagi resurslar), URL preview, import funksiyalari.

Himoya: URL'ni hal qilib IP'ni tekshirish (ichki/rezervlangan diapazonlarni rad etish), DNS rebinding'ga qarshi tekshirilgan IP'ga ulanish, faqat https, redirect'larga ergashmaslik, allowlist (iloji bo'lsa), egress firewall/proxy, bulutda IMDSv2.

$host = parse_url($url, PHP_URL_HOST);
$ip = gethostbyname($host);
abort_if(! filter_var($ip, FILTER_VALIDATE_IP, FILTER_FLAG_NO_PRIV_RANGE | FILTER_FLAG_NO_RES_RANGE), 422);
Http::withOptions(['allow_redirects' => false])->timeout(5)->get($url);
51.9 Broken Access Control Middle

Broken Access Control — OWASP #1: foydalanuvchi ruxsatsiz ma'lumot yoki funksiyaga kira oladi. Ko'rinishlari: IDOR (boshqa foydalanuvchi resursi), funksiya darajasida tekshiruv yo'q (oddiy foydalanuvchi admin endpoint'iga), mass assignment orqali rol o'zgartirish, faqat UI'da yashirish, JWT/cookie manipulyatsiyasi, CORS noto'g'ri sozlanishi, ro'yxatlarda filtrsiz ma'lumot.

Himoya: deny by default, har endpoint'da policy, obyekt darajasida tekshiruv, so'rovlarni foydalanuvchi doirasidan boshlash, avtorizatsiya testlari (har endpoint uchun "begona foydalanuvchi" testi), log va monitoring (ko'p 403 — hujum belgisi).

public function show(Request $request, Invoice $invoice)
{
    $this->authorize('view', $invoice);   // har endpoint'da
    return new InvoiceResource($invoice);
}
51.12 File Upload Security Middle

Fayl yuklash xavflari: zararli kod (.php yuklab bajarish — RCE), MIME soxtalashtirish, katta fayllar (DoS), path traversal nomi (../../.env), zip bomb, XSS SVG'da, zararli PDF/Office, ommaviy kirish (maxfiy hujjat ochiq URL'da).

Himoya: kengaytma va MIME'ni mazmun bo'yicha whitelist (mimes:, mimetypes:), hajm limiti, fayl nomini server generatsiya qiladi (store() tasodifiy nom), web root'dan tashqarida yoki S3'da saqlash, yuklash papkasida PHP bajarilmasligi (Nginx), maxfiy fayllar uchun temporary URL, rasmlarni qayta kodlash (metadata va yashirin payload'ni tozalash), antivirus (ClamAV) muhim tizimlarda.

$request->validate(['document' => ['required', 'file', 'mimes:pdf,jpg,png', 'max:10240']]);
$path = $request->file('document')->store("documents/{$user->id}", 's3');   // tasodifiy nom, private
51.14 Session Security Middle

Sessiya xavfsizligi: session ID tasodifiy va uzun (Laravel ta'minlaydi), cookie Secure, HttpOnly, SameSite; login'dan keyin regenerate() (session fixation'ga qarshi), logout'da invalidate() + regenerateToken(), mutlaq va harakatsizlik muddati, parol o'zgarganda boshqa sessiyalarni bekor qilish (Auth::logoutOtherDevices()), session ma'lumotida sezgir narsalar yo'q, ko'p serverda xavfsiz umumiy saqlash.

Qo'shimcha: muhim amallar uchun qayta parol so'rash (password.confirm middleware), shubhali faollikda (IP/qurilma keskin o'zgarishi) qayta autentifikatsiya.

Route::post('/settings/security', ...)->middleware(['auth', 'password.confirm']);
Auth::logoutOtherDevices($request->input('password'));
51.16 Secret Management Middle

Sirlar: DB parollari, API kalitlari, APP_KEY, JWT kalitlari, webhook sirlari, bulut kalitlari. Qoidalar: hech qachon kodda yoki git'da (.env ignore; tarixda topilsa — darhol almashtirish), log va xato xabarlarida yo'q, minimal ko'rinish (faqat kerakli servislar), muntazam aylantirish, sizib chiqqanini aniqlash (secret scanning — gitleaks, GitHub push protection).

Saqlash: secret manager (AWS Secrets Manager, Vault, Doppler), CI secret'lari, Laravel env:encrypt, K8s secret'lari (shifrlangan etcd bilan). APP_KEY o'zgarsa — shifrlangan ma'lumotlar o'qilmay qoladi (APP_PREVIOUS_KEYS bilan aylantirish).

gitleaks detect --source . --redact
php artisan env:encrypt --env=production
51.18 Security Headers Middle

Xavfsizlik header'lari brauzerga qo'shimcha himoya ko'rsatmalari beradi: HSTS (faqat HTTPS), Content-Security-Policy (qaysi manbalardan skript/stil yuklash mumkin — XSS ta'sirini cheklaydi), X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff, X-Frame-Options/CSP frame-ancestors (clickjacking), Referrer-Policy, Permissions-Policy, Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy.

Laravel'da middleware yoki Nginx orqali; CSP uchun nonce (Vite::useCspNonce()) inline skriptlar bilan. CSP'ni avval Report-Only rejimida joriy qilib, buzilishlarni yig'ish, keyin majburiy qilish.

final class SecurityHeaders
{
    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next): Response
    {
        $response = $next($request);
        $response->headers->set('X-Content-Type-Options', 'nosniff');
        $response->headers->set('Referrer-Policy', 'strict-origin-when-cross-origin');
        $response->headers->set('Content-Security-Policy', "default-src 'self'; script-src 'self' 'nonce-".Vite::cspNonce()."'");
        return $response;
    }
}
51.20 Dependency Vulnerabilities Middle

Uchinchi tomon paketlari — kodingizning katta qismi va hujum yo'li (zaif versiyalar, buzilgan paketlar — supply chain). Himoya: composer audit va npm audit CI'da (kritik zaiflikda build'ni to'xtatish), Dependabot/Renovate avtomatik yangilanishlari, roave/security-advisories, lock fayllar, yangi paket qo'shishdan oldin baholash, ishlatilmagan paketlarni olib tashlash, PHP va Laravel versiyalari qo'llab-quvvatlanadigan.

Zaiflik e'lon qilinganda: ta'sir tahlili (zaif funksiya ishlatiladimi?), SLA bo'yicha yangilash, vaqtinchalik choralar (WAF qoidasi).

composer audit --locked --format=summary
npm audit --omit=dev --audit-level=high
51.23 Secure Error Handling Middle

Xato xabarlari hujumchiga ma'lumot bermasligi kerak: production'da APP_DEBUG=false (stack trace, .env qiymatlari, SQL so'rovlari ko'rinmasin — Ignition debug sahifasi juda xavfli), umumiy xabar + request_id foydalanuvchiga, batafsil — faqat log'ga. Autentifikatsiyada bir xil xabar ("email yoki parol noto'g'ri") — enumeration'ga qarshi.

Xatolar "fail secure" bo'lishi kerak: avtorizatsiya tekshiruvida exception — rad etish, ruxsat berish emas. Exception'larni yutib yubormaslik (jim xatolar xavfsizlik muammolarini yashiradi).

// .env (production)
APP_DEBUG=false
APP_ENV=production

->withExceptions(function (Exceptions $e) {
    $e->render(fn (Throwable $ex, Request $r) => $r->expectsJson() && ! config('app.debug')
        ? response()->json(['message' => 'Serverda xatolik', 'request_id' => $r->header('X-Request-Id')], 500)
        : null);
})

Intervyu savollari

  • senior SQL Injection'ning ikkinchi darajali turi (Second-Order SQLi) nima va PDO Prepared Statements unda qanday ishlaydi?
  • senior Security: SSRF (Server-Side Request Forgery) hujumi nima va Laravel ilovasida webhook yoki rasm yuklashda undan qanday himoyalanamiz?
  • senior Security: ReDoS (Regular Expression Denial of Service) nima va xavfli regex qanday qilib serverni to'xtatib qo'yishi mumkin?
  • middle Laravel Rate Limiting: ThrottleRequests middleware'i qanday ishlaydi va Redis bilan distributed limit qanday o'rnatiladi?
  • middle Security: CORS (Cross-Origin Resource Sharing) qanday ishlaydi va Preflight (OPTIONS) so'rovi nega yuboriladi?
  • middle Security: Session Fixation va Session Hijacking: SameSite cookies va regeneratsiya qanday himoyalaydi?
  • senior Security: Parollarni xeshlash: Argon2id nima uchun Bcrypt'dan xavfsizroq va GPU/ASIC hujumlaridan qanday himoyalaydi?
  • senior Security: PII (Personally Identifiable Information) ma'lumotlarni bazada saqlashda Enkripsiya (Encryption at Rest) qanday qilinadi?
  • middle PHP: serialize() va unserialize() nega xavfli va Insecure Deserialization qanday qilib RCE ga olib kelishi mumkin?
  • middle OWASP: Broken Object Level Authorization (BOLA / IDOR) nima va Laravelda Route Model Binding orqali qanday oldi olinadi?
  • senior Security: Zero Trust Architecture: Mikroxizmatlar aro mTLS (Mutual TLS) va qisqa umr ko'ruvchi tokenlar qanday ishlaydi?

52. OWASP Top 10

52.1 Broken Access Control Middle

Foydalanuvchilar o'z huquqlaridan tashqariga chiqa olishi: IDOR/BOLA, funksiya darajasidagi tekshiruv yo'qligi, rol o'zgartirish (mass assignment, JWT manipulyatsiyasi), CORS noto'g'ri sozlanishi, ro'yxatlarda begona ma'lumot, majburiy browsing (/admin ga to'g'ridan-to'g'ri), SSRF ham 2025'da shu toifaga kiritilgan.

Laravel'da himoya: har endpoint'da policy (authorize, can middleware), so'rovlarni foydalanuvchi/tenant doirasidan boshlash, scoped binding, $fillable + validated(), deny by default, avtorizatsiya testlari, 403 hodisalarini loglash va kuzatish.

Route::middleware('auth')->scopeBindings()->group(function () {
    Route::get('/teams/{team}/projects/{project}', [ProjectController::class, 'show'])->can('view', 'project');
});
52.2 Security Misconfiguration Middle

Noto'g'ri konfiguratsiya: APP_DEBUG=true production'da, default parollar, keraksiz yoqilgan funksiyalar va portlar (ochiq Redis, phpMyAdmin, Telescope/Horizon himoyasiz), noto'g'ri fayl ruxsatlari (.env web orqali ochiq — root public/ emas), xavfsizlik header'lari yo'q, batafsil xato xabarlari, eskirgan dasturlar, noto'g'ri CORS, ochiq S3 bucket'lar, XML parser'larda XXE.

Himoya: takrorlanadigan qattiqlashtirilgan konfiguratsiya (IaC, Docker image), muhitlarni avtomatik tekshirish (php artisan about, CI'dagi konfiguratsiya testlari), minimal o'rnatish, muntazam audit va skanerlash.

it('has safe production configuration', function () {
    config(['app.env' => 'production']);
    expect(config('app.debug'))->toBeFalse()
        ->and(config('session.secure'))->toBeTrue();
});
52.4 Cryptographic Failures Middle

Kriptografik xatolar — sezgir ma'lumotning himoyasizligi: HTTP orqali uzatish, ochiq matnda saqlash (parollar, karta ma'lumotlari, shaxsiy ma'lumotlar), zaif algoritmlar (MD5, SHA-1, DES, ECB rejimi), zaif yoki kodda qattiq yozilgan kalitlar, tasodifiy bo'lmagan qiymatlar (rand() token uchun), sertifikat tekshiruvini o'chirish (verify => false).

Laravel'da: HTTPS hamma joyda, Hash (bcrypt/argon2), Crypt/encrypted cast (AES-256-GCM/CBC + MAC), Str::random()/random_bytes() (kriptografik tasodifiylik), kalitlar secret manager'da, tashqi HTTP'da sertifikat tekshiruvi yoqiq.

$token = Str::random(64);                         // ✅ CSPRNG
$code = random_int(100000, 999999);               // ✅ OTP
// ❌ md5(uniqid()), rand(), Http::withoutVerifying()
52.7 Authentication Failures Middle

Autentifikatsiya xatolari: brute-force va credential stuffing'ga himoya yo'q, zaif parollarga ruxsat, MFA yo'q, xavfsiz bo'lmagan parol tiklash (taxmin qilinadigan token, muddatsiz), session ID URL'da, login'dan keyin session yangilanmaydi, logout'da sessiya bekor qilinmaydi, foydalanuvchi mavjudligini oshkor qiluvchi xabarlar.

Laravel'da: Fortify/Breeze (sinalgan oqimlar), rate limiting, Password::uncompromised(), MFA (Fortify 2FA), parol tiklash token'lari muddatli va bir martalik (default), session()->regenerate(), bir xil xato xabarlari.

Password::defaults(fn () => Password::min(12)->uncompromised());
// config/auth.php → 'passwords.users.expire' => 30, 'throttle' => 60

Intervyu savollari

  • senior Security: Content Security Policy (CSP) nima va u XSS (Cross-Site Scripting) hujumlarini qanday qilib ildizidan yo'q qiladi?
  • senior Security: Blind SQL Injection va Second-Order SQL Injection nima va ularning xavfi nimada?

Performance va kesh

53. Application Performance Optimization

53.1 Performance Profiling Middle

Profiling — kod qayerda vaqt va resurs sarflashini o'lchash. Darajalar: APM (production'da so'rovlar, tranzaksiyalar, DB va tashqi chaqiruvlar — Sentry Performance, New Relic, Datadog, Inspector), profilerlar (funksiya darajasida — Blackfire, Xdebug profiler, SPX, Tideways), Laravel vositalari (Debugbar, Telescope, Clockwork — so'rovlar va vaqt).

Qoida: optimallashtirishdan oldin profiling — intuitsiya ko'pincha xato qiladi. Production'ga yaqin ma'lumot va yuklama bilan; flame graph'lar eng ko'p vaqt olgan yo'llarni ko'rsatadi.

# SPX (yengil profiler) bilan
SPX_ENABLED=1 SPX_REPORT=full php artisan app:heavy-report
# Blackfire
blackfire curl https://staging.example.uz/api/orders
53.2 Response Time Analysis Middle

Javob vaqtini tahlil qilish: umumiy vaqtni qismlarga ajratish — tarmoq/TLS, Nginx kutish, PHP bootstrap, ilova mantiqi, DB so'rovlari (soni va vaqti), kesh, tashqi API'lar, serializatsiya, view rendering. Har qismning ulushi optimallashtirish yo'nalishini ko'rsatadi.

O'rtacha emas, percentillar (p50, p95, p99) — foydalanuvchilarning eng yomon tajribasini ko'rsatadi. Server-Timing header'i brauzer DevTools'da backend bosqichlarini ko'rsatadi.

$start = hrtime(true);
$orders = $this->orders->forUser($user);
$dbMs = (hrtime(true) - $start) / 1e6;
return response()->json($data)->header('Server-Timing', "db;dur={$dbMs}, app;dur=".round((microtime(true) - LARAVEL_START) * 1000));
53.6 OPcache Middle

OPcache — PHP performance'ining eng katta "bepul" yutug'i: kompilyatsiya qilingan opcode'larni shared memory'da saqlaydi, har so'rovda parse va kompilyatsiyani yo'q qiladi (2–3x tezlik). Production sozlamalari: yetarli xotira va fayllar soni, validate_timestamps=0 + deploy'da reload, preloading (ixtiyoriy).

JIT (PHP 8) — CPU-og'ir kod (matematik hisob, rasm qayta ishlash) uchun foyda beradi; odatiy I/O-og'ir web ilovada kam ta'sir. OPcache holatini kuzatish: hit rate (~99%+), xotira to'lishi, restart'lar.

$s = opcache_get_status(false);
printf("hit rate: %.2f%%, used: %d MB, full: %s\n",
    $s['opcache_statistics']['opcache_hit_rate'],
    $s['memory_usage']['used_memory'] / 1048576,
    $s['cache_full'] ? 'ha' : 'yo\'q');
53.8 Eloquent Query Optimization Middle

Eloquent so'rovlarini tezlashtirish: faqat kerakli ustunlar (select), eager loading (with, withCount), agregatlar DB'da (sum, count), katta hajm — lazy()/chunkById(), ommaviy amallar (upsert, query update), subquery select'lar, exists(), indekslar so'rovlarga mos, hydration'dan qochish (hisobotlar uchun toBase()/query builder).

Har murakkab so'rovning SQL'ini va rejasini tekshirish; so'rovlar sonini test bilan cheklash.

$customers = Customer::query()
    ->select(['id', 'name'])
    ->withCount(['orders as paid_orders_count' => fn ($q) => $q->where('status', 'paid')])
    ->withSum(['orders as revenue' => fn ($q) => $q->where('status', 'paid')], 'total')
    ->orderByDesc('revenue')
    ->limit(20)
    ->get();
53.10 Redis Caching Middle

Redis keshlash — qimmat hisob-kitob va so'rovlar natijasini xotirada saqlash: Cache::remember(), tag'lar (Cache::tags() — guruhli invalidatsiya), Cache::flexible() (stale-while-revalidate), Cache::memo() (so'rov ichidagi takroriy o'qishlar uchun), atomik lock'lar.

Nimani keshlash: ma'lumotnomalar, konfiguratsiyalar, og'ir agregatlar, tashqi API javoblari, hisoblangan sahifa bloklari. Kesh kalitlari aniq va versiyalangan, TTL va invalidatsiya rejasi bilan, hit ratio kuzatiladi.

$categories = Cache::tags(['catalog'])->remember('categories:tree', now()->addHour(),
    fn () => Category::with('children')->whereNull('parent_id')->get());
// Kategoriya o'zgarganda
Cache::tags(['catalog'])->flush();
53.11 Queue Offloading Middle

So'rovdan og'ir yoki sekin ishlarni navbatga ko'chirish: email va bildirishnomalar, PDF/Excel generatsiya, rasm qayta ishlash, tashqi API sinxronizatsiyasi, statistika yangilash, webhook yuborish. So'rov tez javob beradi (202 Accepted yoki darhol natija), ish fonda.

Laravel: ShouldQueue (job, listener, mail, notification), dispatch()->afterResponse() (javob yuborilgandan keyin shu jarayonda — yengil ishlar uchun, navbatsiz), defer() (Laravel 11+ — javobdan keyin bajarish).

// Yengil ish — javobdan keyin, navbatsiz
defer(fn () => Metrics::increment('orders.created'));
// Og'ir ish — navbatda
GenerateInvoicePdf::dispatch($invoice)->onQueue('documents');
return response()->json(['status' => 'queued'], 202);
53.20 Latency Percentiles: p50, p95, p99 Middle

O'rtacha qiymat chalg'itadi: 99 ta 50 ms va bitta 5 s so'rov — o'rtacha 100 ms, lekin har 100-foydalanuvchi 5 s kutadi. Percentillar: p50 (median — odatiy tajriba), p95 (20 tadan 1 tasi bundan sekin), p99 (100 tadan 1 tasi — "dum" kechikish), p99.9. Bir sahifa ko'p so'rov qilsa, foydalanuvchi p99'ga tez-tez duch keladi.

SLO'lar percentil bilan ifodalanadi ("p95 < 300 ms"). Percentillarni o'rtachalash mumkin emas — histogrammalardan hisoblang (Prometheus histogram_quantile).

histogram_quantile(0.95, sum(rate(http_request_duration_seconds_bucket{route="/api/orders"}[5m])) by (le))

Intervyu savollari

  • senior Laravel Octane qanday ishlaydi va State Leak (holat sizishi) qanday xavf tug'diradi?
  • senior Laravel Octane (FrankenPHP / Swoole) an'anaviy PHP-FPM dan qanday farq qiladi va undagi State Pollution (xotira ifloslanishi) xavfi nima?
  • middle Production da sahifa kecha 300 ms da ochilardi, bugun 5 soniya. Qanday tekshirasiz?

54. Caching Strategies

54.1 Cache-Aside Middle

Cache-aside (lazy loading) — eng keng tarqalgan pattern: ilova avval keshdan o'qiydi; yo'q bo'lsa (miss) — manbadan (DB) olib keshga yozadi; yozishda DB yangilanadi va kesh kaliti o'chiriladi. Laravel Cache::remember() — aynan shu.

Afzalliklari: faqat so'ralgan ma'lumot keshlanadi, kesh ishlamasa tizim DB bilan ishlaydi (sekinroq). Kamchiliklari: birinchi so'rov sekin (sovuq kesh), qisqa nomuvofiqlik oynasi, stampede xavfi.

public function find(int $id): Product
{
    return Cache::remember("product:{$id}:v2", now()->addMinutes(30),
        fn () => Product::with('media')->findOrFail($id));
}
public function update(Product $p, array $data): void
{
    $p->update($data);
    DB::afterCommit(fn () => Cache::forget("product:{$p->id}:v2"));
}
54.5 Cache Keys Middle

Kesh kaliti dizayni: noyob va bashorat qilinadigan (app:product:42:v3), barcha natijaga ta'sir qiluvchi parametrlarni o'z ichiga oladi (tenant, til, valyuta, filtrlar, sahifa, foydalanuvchi ruxsati — aks holda boshqa foydalanuvchiga noto'g'ri ma'lumot!), versiya prefiksi (format o'zgarganda eski keshni bekor qilish uchun), ilova/muhit prefiksi (bir Redis'da bir nechta ilova).

Uzun parametr to'plamlari uchun hash (md5(json_encode($filters))); kalitlarni bir joyda generatsiya qiluvchi helper/class — tarqoq string'lar xatoga moyil.

final class CacheKeys
{
    public static function productList(int $tenantId, string $locale, array $filters, int $page): string
    {
        ksort($filters);
        return sprintf('catalog:v3:t%d:%s:%s:p%d', $tenantId, $locale, md5(json_encode($filters)), $page);
    }
}
54.7 Cache Invalidation Middle

Invalidatsiya strategiyalari: TTL (passiv), aniq o'chirish (yozishda — observer, event, afterCommit), versiyalangan kalitlar (versiyani oshirish — eskisi amalda yo'q), tag'lar (guruhni birga), event asosidagi (ma'lumot o'zgarishi haqidagi event → kesh tozalash, ko'p serverda Pub/Sub orqali lokal keshlarni ham).

Qiyinliklar: bir ma'lumot ko'p kalitda (detal, ro'yxat, qidiruv, agregat) — bog'liqliklarni xaritalash; tranzaksiya commit'idan oldin invalidatsiya → eski ma'lumot qayta keshlanadi; replica lag → eski ma'lumot keshlanadi.

#[ObservedBy(ProductCacheObserver::class)]
final class Product extends Model {}

final class ProductCacheObserver implements ShouldHandleEventsAfterCommit
{
    public function saved(Product $p): void
    {
        Cache::forget("product:{$p->id}:v2");
        Cache::tags(["category:{$p->category_id}"])->flush();
    }
}

Intervyu savollari

  • middle Redis va Memcached o'rtasidagi asosiy arxitekturaviy farqlar nima va qachon sof Memcached tanlanadi?
  • senior Cache-Aside Pattern da Cache Invalidation va Dual-Write muammosi (DB va Kesh nomutanosibligi) qanday hal qilinadi?
  • middle Laravel: Cache Tags qachon ishlatiladi va nima uchun File/Database kesh drayverlarida Cache Tags ishlamaydi?
  • senior Caching: Cache-Aside vs Write-Through vs Write-Behind kesh strategiyalarining farqlari va xavflari?
  • senior CDN Caching: Stale-While-Revalidate va Cache-Control sarlavhalari orqali statik va dinamik sahifalar qanday keshlanadi?

12. Redis

12.4 Cache-Aside Pattern Middle

Cache-aside (lazy loading): ilova avval cache'dan o'qiydi; topilmasa — DB'dan olib, cache'ga yozadi. Yozishda DB yangilanadi va cache kaliti o'chiriladi (yangilanmaydi — race condition'lar kamroq).

Laravel'da Cache::remember() aynan shu pattern. Afzalliklari: sodda, faqat kerakli ma'lumot keshlanadi, cache ishlamasa tizim DB bilan davom etadi. Kamchiliklari: birinchi so'rov sekin, cache va DB qisqa vaqt nomuvofiq bo'lishi mumkin.

$product = Cache::remember("product:{$id}", 600, fn () => Product::with('images')->findOrFail($id));

// Yangilashda
$product->update($data);
Cache::forget("product:{$id}");
12.5 Cache Invalidation Middle

"Kompyuter fanidagi ikki qiyin muammodan biri." Strategiyalar: TTL (eng sodda — ma'lum eskirishga rozilik), yozishda aniq o'chirish (event/observer orqali), versiyalangan kalitlar (product:42:v7 — versiyani oshirish eski kalitni amalda bekor qiladi), cache tag'lari (guruhni birga o'chirish).

Muammolar: bir ma'lumot ko'p kalitda (ro'yxat, detal, qidiruv) — hammasini topish qiyin; tranzaksiya commit'idan oldin invalidatsiya — eski ma'lumot qayta keshlanadi (afterCommit). Redis'da KEYS pattern bilan o'chirish — xavfli.

// Commit'dan keyin invalidatsiya
DB::afterCommit(fn () => Cache::forget("product:{$product->id}"));
12.7 Distributed Locks Senior

Bir nechta server/worker o'rtasida eksklyuziv bajarishni ta'minlash: SET lock:key token NX PX 30000 (atomik "agar yo'q bo'lsa, muddat bilan yoz"), o'chirishda faqat o'z token'ini tekshirib (Lua bilan atomik) bo'shatish.

Laravel: Cache::lock('report', 30)->block(5, fn () => ...), ->owner(), restoreLock(). Nozikliklar: lock muddati ish davomidan uzun bo'lishi kerak (aks holda ikki jarayon birga ishlaydi), GC pauzasi va tarmoq kechikishi xavfi; to'liq kafolat uchun fencing token yoki DB lock. Redlock algoritmi munozarali — muhim moliyaviy operatsiyalarda DB tranzaksiyasi/unique constraint ishonchliroq.

Cache::lock("invoice:{$order->id}", 30)->block(5, function () use ($order) {
    $this->issueInvoice($order);
});
12.8 Atomic Operations Middle

Redis bitta thread'da buyruqlarni ketma-ket bajaradi — har bir buyruq atomik. INCR, DECRBY, HINCRBY, SETNX, GETDEL, LPOP kabi buyruqlar "o'qi-o'zgartir-yoz" race condition'larsiz ishlaydi.

Klassik xato: PHP'da $v = Redis::get('x'); Redis::set('x', $v + 1); — ikki parallel so'rovda yo'qolgan yangilanish. To'g'risi — Redis::incr('x'). Bir nechta buyruqni atomik qilish uchun MULTI/EXEC yoki Lua skript.

$views = Redis::incr("post:{$id}:views");
if (Redis::setnx("welcome-sent:{$userId}", 1)) {
    Redis::expire("welcome-sent:{$userId}", 86400);
    // faqat bir marta yuboriladi
}
12.11 Redis Queues Middle

Laravel Redis queue driver'i List'lar (queues:default) va Sorted Set'lardan (:delayed, :reserved — muddat bilan) foydalanadi. Worker job'ni olganda u reserved to'plamga o'tadi; retry_after ichida tugallanmasa — qayta navbatga qaytadi.

Muhim sozlama: retry_after har doim eng uzun job timeout'idan katta bo'lishi kerak, aks holda job ikki marta bajariladi. Redis'ning persistence sozlamasi job yo'qolishiga ta'sir qiladi. Horizon Redis queue'larni boshqaradi va monitoring beradi.

// config/queue.php
'redis' => [
    'driver' => 'redis',
    'queue' => 'default',
    'retry_after' => 190,   // eng uzun job timeout (180) + zaxira
    'block_for' => 5,
],
12.13 Rate Limiting Middle

Redis tez atomik hisoblagichlari tufayli rate limiting uchun ideal. Algoritmlar: fixed window (INCR + EXPIRE — sodda, chegarada portlash), sliding window log (Sorted Set — aniq, xotira ko'p), sliding window counter, token bucket (Lua bilan).

Laravel: RateLimiter::for('api', fn ($r) => Limit::perMinute(60)->by($r->user()?->id ?: $r->ip())) va throttle:api middleware; RateLimiter::attempt() ixtiyoriy amallar uchun. Javobda 429 va Retry-After header.

RateLimiter::for('login', fn (Request $r) => [
    Limit::perMinute(5)->by($r->input('email').'|'.$r->ip()),
    Limit::perMinute(100)->by($r->ip()),
]);

Intervyu savollari

  • senior Redis'da Cache Stampede (Dog-piling / Thundering Herd) muammosi nima va unga qarshi qanday kurashiladi?
  • middle Redis Eviction Policy'lar (noeviction, allkeys-lru, volatile-lru) qanday farqlanadi va maxmemory to'lganda nima yuz beradi?
  • senior Redis Sorted Sets (ZSET) yordamida Sliding Window Rate Limiter algoritmi qanday amalga oshiriladi?
  • middle Laravel Cache Tagging (Cache::tags) qanday ishlaydi va nima uchun Redis kesh teglarini me'yordan ortiq ishlatish sekinlashuvga olib keladi?
  • senior Redis: Redis Streams vs Pub/Sub: Xabarlar yo'qolib ketmasligini (Persistence) ta'minlashda qaysi biri to'g'ri?
  • senior Redis Eviction Policies: 'maxmemory' to'lganda 'volatile-lru', 'allkeys-lru' va 'noeviction' strategiyalari qanday ishlaydi?
  • senior Redis: MULTI/EXEC tranzaksiyalari va Lua skriptlarining atomarlikdagi farqi nima?
  • middle Redis: Hashes vs Strings: Foydalanuvchi profilini saqlashda nima uchun HSET oddiy JSON SET ga qaraganda ancha kam xotira egallaydi?
  • middle Xatoni toping: yuzlab foydalanuvchi bir vaqtda like bosganda bu hisoblagich nega noto'g'ri sanaydi?

Debugging va monitoring

56. Debugging

56.12 Root Cause Analysis Middle

Ildiz sabab — simptom emas, xatoning asl manbai: "500 xato" (simptom) ← "null'ga metod chaqiruvi" ← "relation bo'sh" ← "import yozuvni FK'siz yaratgan" ← "import validatsiyasi yo'q" (ildiz). Faqat simptomni tuzatish (null tekshiruvi qo'shish) — xato boshqa joyda qaytadi.

Usullar: 5 Why (har javobga "nega?" — odatda 3–5 qadam), Ishikawa (baliq skeleti — sabab toifalari: kod, ma'lumot, infratuzilma, jarayon, odamlar), vaqt chizig'i (nima qachon o'zgardi). Natija: ildiz sababni tuzatish + himoya qatlamlari (constraint, test, monitoring).

Nega 500? → $order->customer null
Nega null? → customer_id mavjud bo'lmagan mijozga ishora qiladi
Nega? → CSV importda mijoz o'chirilgan, FK yo'q
Nega FK yo'q? → eski migratsiyada qo'shilmagan
Ildiz: referensial yaxlitlik DB'da ta'minlanmagan → FK + import validatsiyasi + test

Intervyu savollari

  • middle Sentry'da kuniga 50 marta takrorlanadigan, lokal muhitda qaytarib bo'lmaydigan xato bor. Qanday tekshirasiz?

57. Xdebug

57.17 Production-Safe Configuration Middle

Xdebug production'da o'rnatilmasligi kerak: performance (sezilarli sekinlashuv), xavfsizlik (step debugging porti orqali masofadan kod bajarish va ma'lumot o'qish xavfi, batafsil xato xabarlari), barqarorlik. Production image'da Xdebug yo'qligini CI'da tekshiring.

Production'da muqobillar: APM (Sentry Performance, New Relic, Datadog), sampling profiler'lar (Blackfire, Tideways — production uchun mo'ljallangan, past overhead), log'lar va trace'lar. Staging'da debug kerak bo'lsa — faqat trigger, faqat ichki tarmoq/SSH tunnel, vaqtincha.

FROM php:8.3-fpm-alpine AS base
# ...
FROM base AS dev
RUN pecl install xdebug && docker-php-ext-enable xdebug
FROM base AS production      # Xdebug yo'q
docker run --rm registry/app:sha php -m | grep -qi xdebug && echo "XATO: Xdebug production image'da!" && exit 1 || true

58. Logging & Monitoring

58.2 Structured Logging Middle

Strukturali log — matn emas, maydonli yozuv (JSON): {"timestamp", "level", "message", "context": {...}, "request_id", "user_id"}. Afzalligi: mashina tahlili, aniq filtr va agregatsiya (Loki, Elasticsearch, CloudWatch Insights), maydonlar bo'yicha qidiruv (order_id=42).

Qoidalar: xabar o'zgarmas ("Order placed"), o'zgaruvchilar kontekstda (xabarga string interpolatsiya qilmaslik — guruhlash buziladi); izchil maydon nomlari; Laravel'da JSON formatter (Monolog\Formatter\JsonFormatter) yoki stderr + JSON konteynerlarda.

// config/logging.php
'stderr' => [
    'driver' => 'monolog',
    'handler' => StreamHandler::class,
    'with' => ['stream' => 'php://stderr'],
    'formatter' => Monolog\Formatter\JsonFormatter::class,
],
